## GAP-P12D-152: the target path's own data check replaces NVMA (owner ruling 2026-10-03, "Replace before the cutover")
- Severity: medium · Likelihood: certain
- Status: fixed (2026-10-03, master v2.33, ACIS-02 v1.6, ACIS-05 v1.11)
- Area: core/runtime/feed_check.py (`FeedWatch`), core/runtime/engine.py (`Runtime.feed`, observed on every tick), core/governor/target_gate.py (`entry_refusal`), core/governor/guards.py (`target=True` skips every regime check), core/runtime/target_loop.py and target_wiring.py (`EngineView.feed_refusal`)
- Description: a daily target entry depended on NVMA's 1-minute regime context (its veto, ILLIQUID's σ rule, warm-up and NaN states, and the low-confidence guard), the interference GAP-P12D-147 and PR16I-03 measured. Owner ruling: replace it with a purpose-built check that reads only the tick feed. BUILT: `FeedWatch` refuses an entry with `no_feed`, `stale_feed` (no tick within 2 × 120 s, or a late tick), `feed_warming` (fewer than 50 spreads seen), `wide_spread` (above 3 × the median of the last 600 quoted spreads: session-agnostic, because the 08:00 New York slot sits on a session boundary) or `macro_event`. `RegimeContext.integrity_tradeable` and `CONFIDENCE_VETOES` (v2.30) are removed as dead; `build_targets` no longer takes NVMA. Exits never ask (C-EXE-7).
- Residual: GAP-P12D-153 — NVMA, QIEA and AGON still run for the legacy signal path; their retirement is planned after the AWS move.
- Justification: fixed; it supersedes GAP-P12D-147's veto split and GAP-P12D-150's "keep ILLIQUID, measure".
- Linked: test_a_target_entry_reads_the_feed_check_and_no_regime, test_each_refusal_reads_only_the_tick_feed, test_a_refused_entry_still_sends_its_exit, [[GAP-P12D-147]], [[GAP-P12D-150]], [[GAP-P12D-153]]

## GAP-P12D-151: D1's first real-data looks (2026-10-03) — kill tests it SURVIVED: net annual Sharpe 0.16 (HAC t 0.57) on the 13 FX, but −0.12 (t −0.40) on the 21 instruments, 2013–2023
- Severity: high · Likelihood: certain
- Status: open (2026-10-03: look `000001`, outcome `000002`, both chain heads on GitHub `look-anchor`; owner rulings "LLM-selected, kill test" and "t < −0.5"; what follows is the owner's)
- Area: `scripts/r1_d1_kill_test.py` at `5d75e9e`; `data/validation/looks` and `data/validation/r1_kill_test/d1.json` (copied to `gs://acis-compute-0929-data/validation/2026-10-03/`)
- Description: D1 (EWMAC 16/64, 32/128, 64/256 at equal weight × FDM, capped) on the demo's 13 FX, graded by RSIM from 2012-12-31 to 2023-12-29 (2,867 decision days, 20,079 fills): the research book, swap-free, the declared C8 costs plus each fill's real quoted spread.
  - **Result:** net annual Sharpe **0.165**, HAC t **0.57**: NOT killed (the rule kills below −0.5). A survival credits nothing: D1 is LLM-selected, so only forward data can pass it (§5.6).
  - **Context, P&L-honest:** the passive-long benchmark earned 0.07, and the paired excess is positive; at 2× cost D1 still earns 0.156, so cost is not what holds it back. The plateau is flat (its six neighbours earn 0.14–0.19): no cliff, so no tuning artifact.
  - **The arms (post-hoc, never a selection):** the slow members earned (EWMAC64/256 0.27, BREAKOUT160 0.32) and the fast ones lost (EWMAC16/64 −0.04, BREAKOUT40 −0.12). The best of 12 correlated arms looks better by chance. Choosing the slow members now would be a NEW family-version, LLM-refined after seeing this look, so it too is kill-only on history.
  - **Against the target:** at a Sharpe near 0.16 the 13-FX book is far from the ~0.2%-a-week target, which needs ~0.65–0.85 (GAP-P12D-148). The levers the research named are breadth (metals and indices, [[GAP-P12D-27]]; more independent mechanisms) and the forward record.
- **The second look, D1 on R6-4's 21 instruments (2026-10-03, look `000003`, outcome `000004`, anchored; `scripts/r1_d1_kill_test.py --universe r21` at `ce6c598`, class-equal weights: each FX 1/39, gold and silver 1/6 each, each index 1/18):** net annual Sharpe **−0.120**, HAC t **−0.40**: NOT killed (the line is −0.5), but WORSE than the 13 FX. The passive-long benchmark earned 0.36 (indices and gold rose strongly over 2013–2023; a trend rule that shorts the dips gives that back), the paired excess is negative, and at 2× cost it earns −0.15. Every plateau neighbour is negative (−0.10 to −0.14) and only the slowest members are near zero or positive (EWMAC64/256 +0.06, BREAKOUT160 +0.10). Reading: on history, breadth through THIS rule on metals and indices hurts; it is not a free lever for D1.
- **Owner ruling 2026-10-03: "FX13 only; shelve onboarding (Recommended)".** The demo trades D1 on the 13 FX from the AWS move; the metals/indices engine onboarding ([[GAP-P12D-27]]) waits until a mechanism earns on them in a history kill test; effort goes to the clean forward record and to kill-testing the next mechanism of the edge slate.
- **2026-10-03, stage C ([[GAP-P12D-159]]):** D1-FX13 on the sealed 2024–25 holdout, graded once (look `000027`): net Sharpe −0.07, HAC t −0.10, not killed; the passive long earned 0.97. Survival credits nothing.
- Justification: open: D1-FX13 runs forward (its credit path); the next mechanism's kill test and the forward record are TODO items 2–3.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-127]], [[GAP-P12D-140]], [[GAP-P12D-148]], [[GAP-P12D-27]], [[GAP-P12D-61]]

## GAP-P12D-150: the post-R1.6 dual audit (2026-10-03) — 14 findings (10 internal, 4 external), triaged by the lead
- Severity: medium · Likelihood: certain
- Status: fixed (2026-10-03: every finding re-derived by the lead from the code or re-run; 10 fixed with regression tests, 1 latent recorded on GAP-P12D-03, 3 owner decisions ruled the same day and applied as master v2.31)
- Area: `de96a75..f0d9524` (the R1.6 audit's fixes, master v2.29, v2.30); reports `archive/AUDIT_POST_R1_6_INTERNAL_2026-10-03.md`, `archive/AUDIT_POST_R1_6_EXTERNAL_2026-10-03.md`; brief `archive/AUDIT_BRIEF_POST_R1_6_2026-10-03.md` (owner's go: "Launch both now (Recommended)")
- Description and dispositions:
  - **PR16I-01 (medium): a `--resume` after a restart cleared a restored throttle with no baseline left to re-arm against** (the trade history is in memory). FIXED: the arming baseline's mean/σ is persisted beside `alpha_armed` (`RiskStore` key `alpha_baseline`; `AlphaDecayDetector.baseline`, `restore_armed(armed, baseline)`), and a persisted baseline while not armed reopens the fresh window (`test_a_restart_and_its_resume_keep_the_fresh_window`, `test_the_throttle_and_its_arming_baseline_survive_a_restart_and_resume`). The ledger's "a restart needs nothing persisted" (GAP-P12D-145) was wrong and is corrected there.
  - **PR16I-02 (medium): the fresh window used the baseline at the resume, not the one that armed the throttle, and after 50 trades the decayed pre-resume trades became the baseline.** FIXED: the window judges against the FROZEN arming baseline until the post-resume trades form their own (recent 50 + baseline 50 = 100), and trades before the resume never count again (`test_the_fresh_window_judges_against_the_baseline_that_armed_it`, `test_after_the_window_only_post_resume_trades_count`).
  - **PR16I-03 (medium): keeping ILLIQUID as an integrity gate keeps 72–76% of the slot blocking GAP-P12D-147 was opened about** (synthetic ticks; the generator's proportional spread inflates ILLIQUID, GAP-P12D-148), and the ruling's keep-list did not name it. OWNER DECISION. The spec now marks ILLIQUID as the lead's reading, pending.
  - **PR16I-04 (medium at $10k paper, low on the $100k demo): in-flight closes were not counted, so a flip was judged one lot short and a slot could carry EUR to 33% against the 30% cap.** FIXED: the executor tracks in-flight closes; `build_portfolio` nets them at the held average; a flip's opening leg is judged net of its own closing leg (`EngineView.portfolio(closing)`, `target_gate.close_leg`) (`test_a_flip_in_flight_nets_its_close_so_a_later_entry_cannot_cross_the_currency_cap`, `test_a_flip_judges_its_open_net_of_its_own_close`).
  - **PR16I-05 = PR16E-03 (low–medium): `download_macro.py --live` still required 10-day freshness of the weekly sources,** so the daily service failed Mon–Wed and a fresh host had no AUD file. FIXED: the live run reads the engine's own limits from config through `core/data/rates.stale_days`; the script runs from any directory; the series checks moved to `scripts/macro_checks.py` (A12); the unit and README text corrected (`test_a_weekly_source_is_held_to_its_own_freshness_limit_in_live_mode`).
  - **PR16I-06 (medium/low): the timer-driven slot traded on a regime context and mark of any age,** so a silent feed was never "stale". FIXED: a target entry needs a context at most `2 × regime.cycle_seconds` old (`stale_context`); an exit stays ungated (former `test_an_entry_on_a_stale_regime_context_is_refused_and_an_exit_is_not` (superseded by the feed watch, GAP-P12D-152: `test_each_refusal_reads_only_the_tick_feed`)). The loop test's contexts were 87 days old: they are now explicitly fresh.
  - **PR16I-07 (low): master §2's priority list and ACIS-02 §1/§7 still called the veto absolute.** FIXED: both state the v2.30 target-entry exception (ACIS-02 v1.5).
  - **PR16I-08 (low, A16): five wirings or semantics survived mutation.** FIXED: the shipped-config runtime test asserts the clock, weekly-rate and in-flight wiring (`_assert_the_target_wiring` in `test_a_runtime_from_the_shipped_config_wires_the_target_loop_and_silences_legacy`); the PR16I-02 tests pin the frozen baseline and the window's end; `test_only_the_human_resume_reaches_the_throttles_clear` pins the one caller by AST.
  - **PR16I-09 (low, A13): STATUS, TODO and CLAUDE.md contradicted the spec header and the ledger.** FIXED (master v2.30, the rulings built, the top-up done).
  - **PR16I-10 (low, pre-existing): the target path checked "registered" but not "validated".** FIXED: the loop asks `is_registered and entry_allowed` at the decision and again at the slot, where only opens are dropped (`test_a_family_frozen_after_the_decision_opens_nothing_at_the_slot`); a fresh registration is INCUBATION with entries allowed, checked.
  - **PR16E-01 (medium): the alpha-decay throttle mostly detects noise.** VERIFIED by the lead with the real detector on returns that never decay (200 paths, shipped values): at a per-trade mean/σ of 0.05 / 0.16 / 0.3 it arms within 200 trades in 84% / 93% / 70% of paths and within 300 in 97% / 100% / 91%; after a human resume it falsely re-arms within 49 trades in 70% / 47% / 39%. On a one-lot book an armed throttle zeroes every entry. OWNER DECISION (`alpha_decay_sharpe_mult` is a guardrail value; the semantic is human-only).
  - **PR16E-02 (low): BIS's year-end release gap (16 days, 2025-12-23 to 2026-01-08) plus its ~7-day lag puts AUD at 22 days on 2026-01-07,** past the 21-day limit, so the three AUD pairs give no target for a day or two; the RBA holds no January meeting. OWNER DECISION (the 21 days are ruled).
  - **PR16E-04 (low, latent): the 08:00 New York FX slot sits inside the macro gate's 30-minute lookahead** (US CPI/NFP/GDP 08:30 ET, ECB 08:15 NY): once a live event calendar is wired, target entries are refused on 6 of 13 pairs on US release days and the slot is not retried. Inert today (`macro_imminent=False`). RECORDED on [[GAP-P12D-03]], decided when the calendar is built.
- **Owner rulings 2026-10-03 (all "Recommended"), applied as master v2.31:** PR16E-01 "Report only on demo": on the one-lot paper/practice book the alpha-decay ×0.25 is reported, not applied, to target entries (the loop passes `alpha_throttles = lots.cap is None`; `test_on_the_one_lot_demo_alpha_decay_is_reported_not_applied`, `test_the_slot_reports_alpha_decay_on_the_capped_book_and_applies_it_live`), live keeps it until GATES R8; PR16I-03 "Keep it, measure": ILLIQUID stays a gate, measured by the demo's first two weeks of `TARGET_ENTRY_ZERO`; PR16E-02 "28 days for weekly": `targets.rate_max_stale_days_weekly: 28` (schema le 28, ACIS-04 v1.8).
- Residual: GAP-P12D-61 — GATES R8 (the shadow-book edge monitor) replaces the live throttle with the post-edge plan; PR16E-04 is GAP-P12D-03's, and the ILLIQUID measurement is GAP-P12D-141 (10)'s (TODO START HERE item 3).
- Justification: every finding was re-derived from the code paths cited or re-run (PR16E-01 independently, with the real detector); none moves a guardrail value or the equity floor.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-144]], [[GAP-P12D-145]], [[GAP-P12D-146]], [[GAP-P12D-147]], [[GAP-P12D-148]], [[GAP-P12D-03]]

## GAP-P12D-149: C-BT-11 v2's stage record has no producers yet — B0's record, stage B, stage C and the per-account re-grade — so no report can pass
- Severity: medium · Likelihood: certain
- Status: open (2026-10-03, R1.4; found building GAP-P12D-104)
- Area: tests/backtest/report.py `StageRecord`, tests/backtest/verdict.py (stage A only), ACIS-09 C-BT-11 v2 steps 0, 4, 5, 6
- Description: `passed` now requires a stage record, but only stage A has a producer (`verdict.cycle_verdict`). B0's lab machinery exists (GAP-P12D-105) without a record at a `recon_commit`; stage B (one frozen expanding-WF run of the real engine on hourly bars, graded on daily OOS net returns, Bonferroni over ≤ 3 engine looks, the engine's Sharpe not significantly below RSIM's) is NOT BUILT and had no ledger owner; stage C (GAP-P12D-114) and the per-account re-grade (GAP-P12D-112) are open. Stage B also needs the R1.6 target path inside the backtest harness, since the legacy fast-loop harness grades the retired modules.
- Justification: open and safe: every report fails closed (`passed` is False without a full record), and the live gate reads a rung-2 record that production wires to False. Not needed for the first stage-A look; needed before any family can be called "validated on history".
