The owner also ruled the demo may run unattended before this entry closes. **2026-10-01 (second session):** the stop rule BUILT ([[GAP-P12D-135]] fixed); the owner then ruled the rest of this batch WAITS behind the R1 build ("R1 build on the lab", to use the Google credits before 2026-10-21; TODO START HERE).
- Area: core/runtime/ (engine.py, wiring.py, venue_link.py), core/execution/ctrader_face.py `account_snapshot`, ACIS-13 §7.5, master §10
- Description — each item, and what bounds it today:
  - **(a) Practice equity lags by up to one `equity_poll_s` (300 s).** ACIS-13 §7.5's event-sourced account view is unbuilt: every `account_snapshot()` spends two requests, so Risk judges the last poll between polls. At one minimum lot (~$0.10 a pip on EUR/USD) no 300 s move carries a $10,000 demo near its $9,000 floor, and the venue's stops rest regardless.
  - **(b) TSMOM cannot fire on a fresh runtime.** It needs 260 daily closes (`tsmom_min_history_days`), and a runtime starts with none, so on the demo TSMOM stays silent; the 60 s-bar modules warm within hours. The natural seed is the venue's own D1 history (ACIS-13 budgets a historical request class).
  - **(c) No live event calendar.** NVMA's `macro_imminent` and the Governor's blackout read False in the runtime. The demo rulebook declares `news.restricted: false`; the historical calendar is [[GAP-P12D-03]].
  - **(d) Pair coupling exits are not wired** (`Governor.check_group_exits` needs AGON's coupling state as data). A pair is bounded by its target, the max hold, the orphan close and the venue stops; and at one minimum lot a USD-balanced pair is usually refused whole at Execution.
  - **(e) Loop 1 promotes; it does not evolve.** Live QIEA evolution is not run in-process: the demo's sets are evolved offline and staged ([[GAP-P12D-129]], owner ruling).
  - **(f) Practice needs a human at a terminal after every restart.** No MCP transport carries `acis_set_mode` to a running engine ([[GAP-P12D-08]]), so a supervised restart comes back in paper. Deliberate (a restart is never a relaxation), but it bounds demo continuity.
  - ~~**(g) A hung feed is not a stall the runtime sees.**~~ **BUILT 2026-09-30 with [[GAP-0020]]:** a lost link reconnects; a detached account ends the reconnector, the runtime treats that as a stall (flatten what it can, halt, exit 1), and the engine unit does not restart on the attach's own refusal (exit 2).
  - **(h) A link lost while an order is in flight** used to halt entries (the orchestrator's reconcile raised on the dead link). **Mitigated 2026-09-30 ([[GAP-P12D-132]] TAI-08):** the reconcile's own `ConnectionError` is caught, the fragment stays SENT, and the reconnect's SYNCING reports its fill once as a venue fill. An ack timeout on a live link is tested (`test_an_ack_timeout_through_the_orchestrator_is_searched_and_reported_once`); a runtime test of the dead-link path is still owed here.
  - **(i) BUILT 2026-10-01 (owner go):** `config/watchdog.yaml` binds the cTrader plugin to the demo (48971796, a test checks it matches the engine binding: `test_the_watchdog_binding_matches_the_engine_binding`); ACIS-13 §14.2 case 8 PASSED on the demo (`scripts/ctrader_case8.py`: a frozen engine's position flattened, account proven flat), after fixing [[GAP-P12D-133]]; the watchdog runs supervised on acis-compute-1 (a transient systemd unit, Restart=always; not reboot-proof until the unit is installed). Was: ~~**The watchdog cannot act on the demo** ([[GAP-P12D-132]] TAI-03, TAE-14). `config/watchdog.yaml` is `mock`; the cTrader plugin needs `environment` and `ctid_trader_account_id` (the owner's demo account), an env file with the three `ACIS_CTRADER_*` variables, and in the container a read-only mount of the token store. Before any UNATTENDED practice run: commit that config and run ACIS-13 §14.2 case 8 (the plugin flattens a demo position while the engine is dead). Bounded today: the mock plugin now reports `flatten_failed` rather than a flatten, and the venue stops rest.~~
  - **(j) An adopted position carries the venue's entry price and stop, but not its target, group or original `opened_at`** ([[GAP-P12D-132]] TAI-15). It exits by its venue stop or the max hold, timed from adoption.
  - **(k) A foreign position opened MID-RUN is not seen until the next start** ([[GAP-P12D-132]] TAI-10): the periodic reconcile reads ACIS's positions. Every start refuses one (exit 2).
  - **(l) ACIS-13 §12's error table is not a code map with a test per row** ([[GAP-P12D-132]] TAE-09): the bounded retries for `PENDING_EXECUTION`/`POSITION_LOCKED` are unbuilt. Bounded: such a refusal is a rejected report (no fill), so the next tick decides again.
- Justification: open. None of these weakens a guard: each fails toward fewer entries or a halted, protected book. (a), (f) and (i) matter most for an unattended demo; (g) is built with [[GAP-0020]].
- Linked: [[GAP-0091b]], [[GAP-0099]], [[GAP-0075]], [[GAP-0020]], [[GAP-P12D-08]], [[GAP-P12D-03]], [[GAP-P12D-129]]

## GAP-P12D-130: the orchestrator dropped every order's stop — an engine open reached the venue without `stop_loss`, so cTrader refused it and the paper venue rested none
- Severity: high · Likelihood: certain
- Status: fixed (2026-09-30, found by the lead reading the orchestrator for Track A step 4, the same day)
- Area: [DIR:core/execution] orchestrator.py `_submit_once`; master §279 (every SizedOrder carries a broker-side stop); ACIS-13 §7.3 step 2
- Description: `_submit_once` called `face.submit_order(direction, ioc, **common)` and `common` carried no stop. On the cTrader face every engine open was therefore refused `VENUE_UNPROTECTED_OPEN_REFUSED` (the face's own guard held, so nothing unprotected was ever placed), and on the mock face a paper position rested no stop, so it could never stop out. The conformance suite called faces directly, so no test saw the gap.
- Justification: fixed. An open now passes `stop_loss=order.stop_loss`; a close still carries none (the face contract's reduce). Both tests fail with the fix reverted (checked on the server).
- Proof: `test_an_orchestrated_open_rests_the_orders_own_stop_and_a_close_carries_none` (every adapter), `test_an_open_through_the_orchestrator_carries_its_stop_to_the_venue` (cTrader, fake venue).
- Residual: none — the stop is threaded for every face, and the face's refusal of a stop-less open stays as the backstop.
- Linked: [[GAP-0091b]], [[GAP-P12D-42]]

## GAP-P12D-129: the demo trades nothing until a parameter set is active — NVMA marks every regime untradeable without one, and BASELINE.md forbids a fabricated set
- Severity: high · Likelihood: certain
- Status: open [impl — owner-ruled 2026-09-30] (found by the lead scoping Track A step 4)
- Area: core/optimizer/param_library.py, core/regime/nvma.py `has_params_for`, BASELINE.md, master §5 ("validated": paper and practice at minimum size), tests/backtest/evolve_step.py
- Description: `NVMA(has_params_for=ParamLibrary.has_active)` makes a regime untradeable while the library holds no active set for it, so a runtime pointed at the demo with an empty library never trades. BASELINE.md rules out a hand-made set, and the demo's scope was an open owner decision ([[GAP-P12D-68]] item 9).
- **Owner ruling (2026-09-30, "Evolve pre-2024 (Recommended)"):**
  - run the existing QIEA evolve-driver on the Google server over the 730 days of Dukascopy history ending 2023-12-31, for the venue's mapped instruments (EUR_USD, USD_JPY);
  - stage the champions in the demo's own ParamLibrary;
  - trade them at ONE venue minimum lot per instrument (master §5 v2.22, paper and practice at minimum size; [[GAP-P12D-106]]);
  - it never touches the 2024–25 holdout and writes no graded look, so [[GAP-P12D-127]]'s closed gate is untouched;
  - recorded as unvalidated, as master §5 already says of the as-built demo. The demo proves plumbing, not edge ([[GAP-P12D-49]]).
- Build owed:
  1. a demo-evolve script: the labelled-bar cache and the per-regime evolve over the declared span, staging into the param DB the runtime opens, with the run's record (span, seeds, champions) kept. **BUILT 2026-09-30:** `scripts/evolve_demo_params.py` (one fold of the walk-forward's own `evolve_fold`, `train_end` 2024-01-01 exclusive; the record `params.evolution.json` beside the library says `validated: false`). The server run is owed.
  2. ~~one-minimum-lot sizing in paper and practice modes~~ **BUILT 2026-09-30** as ACIS-05 v1.6 §7 step 3b: the Governor's injected `fixed_units`; a zero is never raised, and the hard limits judge the lot itself. Proof: `test_paper_and_practice_trade_exactly_one_minimum_lot`, `test_the_minimum_lot_never_raises_a_zero_and_meets_every_hard_limit`. The runtime wires it (item 3's build).
  3. ~~the runtime promotes the staged sets at its first session boundary while flat (C2)~~ **BUILT 2026-09-30:** the runtime sweeps crossed boundaries with the shared `BoundaryClock` (moved to `core/runtime/boundaries.py`), and the library's flat gate counts in-flight work as well as held positions. Proof: `test_staged_params_promote_at_a_crossed_session_boundary_while_flat`.
- **The run, 2026-09-30** (Google server, commit `6863267`, 4 instrument-years labelled in ~45–50 min each, evolution ~7 min; the record is `params.evolution.json` beside the library, in `gs://acis-compute-0929-data/runs/2026-09-30-demo-evolve/runtime/`): **ONE champion across nine regimes** — WEAK_TREND_UP, evolved on USD_JPY (holdout E_R 0.606 on only 6 trades, per-trade Sharpe 0.44). Four more candidates passed the holdout and were refused by the §5.6 plateau law (WEAK_TREND_UP/EUR_USD, RANGING_TIGHT on both, MEAN_REVERTING/EUR_USD); every other regime produced no candidate that passed the holdout. So the demo trades only while an instrument is classified WEAK_TREND_UP (~8% of 2022–23's bars) and is otherwise untradeable: rare trades, enough to prove the plumbing. Whether that is demo enough is the owner's call; nothing was loosened to get more.
- Justification: open until the staged set is deployed with the runtime on the demo host (the champion is staged; the runtime promotes it at its first boundary while flat).
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-68]], [[GAP-P12D-106]], [[GAP-P12D-127]], [[GAP-P12D-49]], [[GAP-0091b]]

## GAP-P12D-128: the three-loop runtime had no lawful home — master §10 carried no [DIR] tag, §1 forbids an unmapped directory, and the import contract rejects any new core package
- Severity: medium · Likelihood: certain
- Status: fixed (2026-09-30, owner ruling "core/runtime/ (Recommended)", answering the session's standalone question; applied as master v2.25; found by the lead at the start of Track A step 4)
- Area: master §1 (directory map) and §10; tests/unit/test_import_contract.py; deploy/systemd/acis-engine.service
- Description: the runtime composes all seven layers plus `monitoring/`, so it fits none of the layer packages without breaking the layer law, and `test_no_upward_or_skip_imports` asserts `unknown core layer directory` on any other `core/` package. The deploy template already named `python -m core.runtime` as the entry point, but no spec did.
- Justification: fixed. Master v2.25 maps `core/runtime/ [DIR:core/runtime]` to §10 as the composition root: it may import every layer and `monitoring/`, and no layer may import it. The contract test holds both directions.
- Proof: `test_no_upward_or_skip_imports` (the runtime's entry in ALLOWED), `test_no_layer_imports_the_runtime`.
- Residual: GAP-0091b — the runtime itself (Track A step 4).
- Linked: [[GAP-0091b]], [[GAP-0075]], [[GAP-0099]]

## GAP-P12D-127: the R1.2 dual audit — 26 findings (15 internal, 11 external) on the look register v2; triaged, real-data looks closed until R1.4, both owner decisions ruled
- Severity: high · Likelihood: certain
- Status: mitigated (2026-09-29, triaged the same day; the constitution's two audits of R1.2, launched on the owner's go ("Launch both now (Recommended)"), brief `archive/AUDIT_BRIEF_R1_2_2026-09-29.md` on `192c1fa`; reports `archive/AUDIT_R1_2_INTERNAL_2026-09-29.md` and `archive/AUDIT_R1_2_EXTERNAL_2026-09-29.md`. The lead's three claims held: 1,057 passed, the eight proof tests pass, the register is 290 lines)
- Area: tests/backtest/look_claims.py, look_register.py, look_gate.py, scripts/run_oos_validation.py, the verdict module (R1.4), master §5.6, ACIS-09 C-BT-11 v2 steps 1–3
- Description — the findings, by cluster (the reports are the full log):
  - **The LLM-repeat refusal can be dodged.** By relabelling the origin (R12I-02, E-R12-01, high: an LLM plan re-registers as `human` or `literature`, and literature is exempt on post-publication data) and by respelling (R12I-01, E-R12-08: instrument order, 8 vs 8.0, formula whitespace or case, the universe label; the hash is not RFC 8785).
  - **The cycle's testing order is not enforced as "before any look"** (R12I-04, high; E-R12-05): a cycle registered after its members were graded, winner first, is accepted; order entries are unvalidated; looks do not name their cycle; rolling back the last lines lets the order be rewritten.
  - **The α-spending schedule is not enforced** (R12I-05, E-R12-04): three cycles at 0.05 register a lifetime 0.15 against the spec's 0.03, 0.01, 0.005.
  - **Outcome integrity** (R12I-08, E-R12-06): an outcome can be recorded for a look never graded, with holdout dates on a development look, or from another register; the outcome file and its vector pointer sit outside the hash chain.
  - **The claim is not checked against the run** (R12I-03), and it lacks fields the spec requires at registration (E-R12-03: the look budget k, the passive benchmark, fixed vs explored, the bootstrap block length, the Holm set).
  - **The cost fingerprint omits the swap and financing inputs** (R12I-07).
  - **Register-first is not enforced repo-wide** (R12I-12): `Backtest.run` graded a real Dukascopy hour without a register line.
  - **Rules with no code and no ledger owner** (R12I-11): the graveyard-motif refusal and the declarative-plan shape (master §5.6), and the void rule for a graded number without a register row (C-BT-11 v2).
  - **Hollow proof tests** (R12I-10: six hand-made faults leave them green); a datetime-keyed daily vector stores but never loads (E-R12-09); the quarter clock is local and accepts a timestamp earlier than the line before (E-R12-10); a vector temp-file clash under concurrency (R12I-09); read-path staleness (E-R12-11).
  - **Owner decisions** (spec-level, not the lead's to make): E-R12-02, the origin's cutoff is the wrong date (a reliable-knowledge vs a training-data cutoff, and web-search agents leak past it, so the horizon for this project's proposers is the proposal date; a master §5.6 change); E-R12-07, the register is not tamper-evident to an outside reader (no commitment leaves the machine), which raises [[GAP-P12D-86]]'s severity now that R1.2 feeds the α budget, the cycle order and the LLM refusal.
- **Triage, 2026-09-29 (the lead; every finding dispositioned):**
  - **The structural mitigation first. Real-data looks and cycle rows are CLOSED** (`look_claims.REAL_LOOKS_OPEN = False`) until R1.4 lands with the recording fixes below. This enforces the ratified R-1 rule "no real-data look before R1.1–R1.4 are green", and it means no append-only line can be written that lacks a declaration. Proof: former `test_real_data_looks_and_cycle_rows_are_closed_until_r1_4` (opened, scoped, 2026-10-03: `test_real_looks_are_open_only_for_literature_and_human_claims_graded_by_rsim_or_events`).
  - **FIXED with tests** (`tests/backtest/test_look_claims.py`):
    - R12I-01 / E-R12-08: the plan is hashed normalised (NFC, stripped, integral floats as ints, grid values and instruments as sets, the formula's whitespace collapsed and case-folded); the universe label and case of the variant no longer make a new variant. `test_the_same_plan_respelled_is_the_same_variant`. The mental model now states the exact serialisation instead of "canonical JSON".
    - R12I-02 / E-R12-01: origin is sticky per variant (an LLM variant is refused again under any origin), and model fields on a non-LLM origin are refused. `test_an_llm_variant_is_refused_again_under_any_origin`, `test_an_origin_records_what_the_contamination_law_needs`. The OOS script's usage now models `--origin llm`.
    - R12I-03 (partly): the claim's instruments must be the look's; the engine claim's grid now includes the code commit. `test_a_real_look_must_carry_its_own_familys_claim_and_the_line_records_it`.
    - R12I-04 / E-R12-05 (partly): a cycle names 64-hex variant hashes only, and none may have had a real look. R12I-05 / E-R12-04: the cycle's α is the spending schedule's (0.03, 0.01, 0.005, …) — the lead's first formula gave 0.02 for cycle 2, caught by the new test. R12I-06 / E-R12-10: quarters are UTC calendar quarters, and no line may be stamped before the line before it. `test_a_cycle_orders_unlooked_variants_at_the_spending_alpha_one_a_utc_quarter`.
    - R12I-08 (partly): an outcome is recorded only for a graded look of this register whose line is on disk as written. R12I-09 / E-R12-09: a vector is keyed by dates (never datetimes), written with a unique fsynced temp and an exclusive link, and read back before its hash is returned. `test_the_daily_vector_is_stored_once_by_its_hash_and_an_edit_is_caught`.
    - R12I-10: the hollow proof tests are strengthened (pairwise cost-map inequality, one test per C8 field, six-member cycle, α schedule, model fields read back). `test_the_cost_map_hash_separates_grades_priced_differently`.
    - R12I-13: a line of an unknown kind breaks the scan (fail closed) and counting skips it. `test_a_line_of_a_kind_the_register_cannot_read_fails_it_closed`.
    - R12I-14: fields are stripped, dates normalised, the space canonicalised at construction, foreign fields refused.
    - R12I-15 / E-R12-11: the script's documented commands run; README and STATUS corrected.
  - **MITIGATED by the closed gate, owned by R1.3/R1.4** (they cannot bite while no real look or cycle row can be written):
    - R12I-03's simulator binding: R1.4, before the gate opens. E-R12-03's registration-time declarations BUILT 2026-10-01 (`tests/backtest/look_declarations.py`): the claim carries fixed vs explored, the look budget k, the passive benchmark, and the explored kind's block length and seed, all sticky per variant; a variant's (k+1)-th real look in a tier is refused; the cycle row carries its Holm set (chain + Holm <= 5). Proof: `test_a_claim_declares_its_kind_budget_and_benchmark_and_the_line_records_them`, `test_a_declaration_is_sticky_and_the_look_budget_binds_per_tier`, `test_a_cycle_registers_its_holm_set_outside_the_chain`.
    - R12I-04 / E-R12-05's `cycle_id` and look index on each stage-A look: R1.4.
    - R12I-07's financing fingerprint in the cost map: R1.3 (the C8 swap table).
    - R12I-08 / E-R12-06's outcome as a chained line, verified by `scan`: R1.4.
    - R12I-11's graveyard-motif refusal, declarative-plan schema and void rule: R1.3 (the plan schema) and R1.4 (the motif veto, the void rule).
    - R12I-12's ungated `Backtest.run`: R1.4's `evaluate()` becomes the only producer of a graded number, with a required consumed-look token (GAP-P12D-98's residual).
  - **OWNER DECISIONS, RULED 2026-09-29:** E-R12-02 "The stricter boundary (Recommended)" — applied as master v2.24 §5.6 and ACIS-09 v1.10, and BUILT: `Origin` records the training-data cutoff, the proposal date and the exposure, an LLM-selected paper is LLM-touched, and the claim line records its certifying boundary (`test_the_certifying_boundary_is_the_later_of_the_cutoff_and_an_exposed_proposal`). E-R12-07 "Push the chain head to GitHub (Recommended)" — applied as ACIS-09 v1.10; built at R1.4 ([[GAP-P12D-86]]).
- Justification: mitigated; the gate stays closed until the R1.3/R1.4 items and the GitHub anchor land. No real-data look has been registered (`data/validation/looks` does not exist), so nothing has been graded wrongly.
- **Progress 2026-10-03 (R1.4):** the verdict module (stage A's chain, [[GAP-P12D-111]]), `passed` ([[GAP-P12D-104]]) and the portfolio unit ([[GAP-P12D-100]]) are BUILT. Also BUILT the same day: R12I-03, `begin_grade(simulator=...)` refuses a claim naming another grader (`test_a_look_claiming_one_simulator_cannot_be_graded_by_the_other`); R12I-04, a stage-A look (tier 2, rsim) must belong to a registered cycle and its line names the cycle and look index, and the verdict counts it only there (`tests/backtest/look_cycles.py`, `test_a_stage_a_look_names_its_cycle_and_counts_only_there`); R12I-08, the outcome is a `kind: outcome` line of the hash chain (`tests/backtest/look_outcomes.py`; one per look; an outcome naming no earlier look breaks the scan; `test_the_outcome_is_a_chained_line_and_an_edit_or_a_second_outcome_is_caught`, `test_an_outcome_line_naming_no_earlier_look_breaks_the_register`). **OPENED 2026-10-03 (owner: "Open for literature/human (Recommended)"): `REAL_LOOKS_OPEN = True`, scoped by `OPEN_ORIGINS` (human, literature) and `OPEN_SIMULATORS` (rsim) in `look_claims.admit_claim` (`test_real_looks_are_open_only_for_literature_and_human_claims_graded_by_rsim_or_events`); the verdict counts toward a pass only days after a claim's certifying boundary, and a look wholly before it is kill-only (no cycle, no α, `verdict.kill_verdict` at HAC t < −0.5; `test_only_days_after_the_certifying_boundary_count_toward_a_pass`, `test_a_kill_only_look_names_no_cycle_and_is_killed_only_below_minus_half`).** The anchor ([[GAP-P12D-86]]) is BUILT the same day. Still owed for the scope's two closed doors (LLM plans, engine looks): R12I-11 (the motif veto, the LLM plan schema, the void rule), R12I-12 (`Backtest.run` grades without a consumed-look token), the owner's standing approval for the anchor's push per register line, and the α design ([[GAP-P12D-140]] (1)).
- Residual: GAP-P12D-127 — R12I-11 (the motif veto, the LLM plan schema, the void rule) before LLM plans may register, and R12I-12 (`Backtest.run`'s consumed-look token) before engine looks may.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-98]], [[GAP-P12D-64]], [[GAP-P12D-66]], [[GAP-P12D-86]], [[GAP-P12D-89]], [[GAP-P12D-111]]

## GAP-P12D-126: building the cTrader adapter found five defects in ACIS-13 v1.0's text; the build follows the corrected behaviour, and the spec text needs a v1.1
- Severity: medium · Likelihood: high
- Status: fixed (2026-09-29: the owner ratified "Ratify both (Recommended)", applied as ACIS-13 v1.1 the same day; found by the lead building Track A step 3 the day ACIS-13 locked)
- Area: specs/ACIS-13-VENUE_ADAPTERS.md §4.1, §7.1 A7–A8, §7.3 step 6, §7.8, §8.1 vs §12; core/execution/ctrader_sync.py, core/data/ctrader/session.py, core/data/ctrader/feed.py
- Description:
  - **(a) The restart order double-counts (§7.8, §7.1 A7).** The spec ingests missed deals BEFORE resolving the fragments in flight at the crash. A crashed open's own fill would then reach Risk twice: once as "unsolicited", once when its fragment resolves. Built: fragments first, then only deals of orders ACIS did not send. Proof: `test_an_open_in_flight_at_the_crash_is_reported_once_under_its_own_order`, `test_a_stop_that_fires_while_acis_is_down_reaches_risk_once_at_restart`.
  - **(b) A close cannot be found by `clientOrderId` (§7.3 step 6).** `ProtoOAClosePositionReq` carries none. Built: a close is recorded per venue position before it leaves, and an unknown outcome is searched as the MARKET closing order on that position, skipping orders already recorded as ACIS's. Proof: `test_a_close_whose_reply_is_lost_is_found_after_a_restart_never_sent_twice`.
  - **(c) §8.1 and §12 disagree on `ProtoOAClientDisconnectEvent`** (DISCONNECTED and backoff, vs DETACHED). Built: a disconnect; an application refused on the reconnect's authorisation detaches — true only since [[GAP-P12D-132]] TAE-09 (2026-09-30); before that the reconnect retried a refused application without end (`test_an_application_refused_on_reconnect_detaches_and_ends_the_task`).
  - **(d) §4.1 says `RawTick.volume` is omitted,** but the locked ACIS-01 type requires it. Built: 0.0, with `quote_volume = False` in the venue profile.
  - **(e) The A8 clock check needs a live tick,** so it cannot finish inside the attach; it is the runtime's first-live-tick check ([[GAP-0091b]]), and entries stay blocked until it passes.
- Justification: fixed. ACIS-13 v1.1 states each correction; the code implemented them first, with the tests named above. Every correction makes the adapter stricter or exact-once; none touches a guardrail, HALT/veto semantics or the equity floor. The `pepperstone_demo` rulebook was ratified in the same answer.
- Residual: none — (e), the first-live-tick clock check, is BUILT in the runtime (2026-09-30, `Runtime._clock_ok`): an instrument takes no entry until one live tick arrives within `clock_skew_tolerance_ms`, and its exits are never blocked. Proof: `test_no_entry_until_a_live_tick_proves_the_host_clock`.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-70]], [[GAP-P12D-69]], [[GAP-0091b]], [[GAP-0020]]

## GAP-P12D-125: the R1.1 dual audit — 23 findings (14 internal, 9 external), every one triaged; the two owner decisions ratified
- Severity: medium-high · Likelihood: certain
- Status: fixed (2026-09-29, the same day: the exact split re-measured, both owner decisions ratified. The constitution's two audits of R1.1, launched on the owner's go, brief `archive/AUDIT_BRIEF_R1_1_2026-09-29.md` on `b5c284a`; reports `archive/AUDIT_R1_1_INTERNAL_2026-09-29.md` and `archive/AUDIT_R1_1_EXTERNAL_2026-09-29.md`; triaged in-session the same day, every fix run on the Google server)
- Area: the R1.1 lab (`tests/backtest/lab_*.py`), the tick generator, the oracle ladder, the cost-stress ladder, B0, the read path
- Description — each finding and its disposition:
  - **R11I-01 (medium-high) — the "~98% direction" split was a regression artifact.** FIXED in code: `ClosedTrade` now carries its exact `friction` and `carry`, the attribution slices sum them, and the ladder's `decompose` states the exact 1x split and refuses to estimate one. The claims in GAP-P12D-49/124, CLAUDE.md, STATUS.md and TODO.md are corrected. Proof: `test_a_closed_trade_carries_its_exact_friction_and_carry`, `test_the_decomposition_is_exact_and_refuses_to_estimate`. The re-run with the new code measured it exactly: 1x friction $15.65, 22% of the loss (GAP-P12D-49).
  - **R11I-02 / E-R11-02 (medium) — Cont's β gate passed on its four pinned seeds (β̄ 0.405–0.417).** FIXED: knobs re-tuned on the server (ν 4.5, GARCH (0.02, 0.90), log-vol sd 0.6, half-lives 5…3,125): β̄ 0.358 ± 0.006 over 40 seeds, 10% of 4-seed blocks failing (was 60–74%). The gate is now a 24-seed mean with a 2 se margin; numpy is pinned to 2.4.x. Proof: `test_8_absolute_return_autocorrelation_decays_as_a_power_law`.
  - **R11I-03 / E-R11-01 (medium / medium-high) — B0 depends on the seed block and the path count.** MITIGATED: B0 now grades the rule its closed form describes (sign-EWMAC on y), on the declared block B0_N = 2,000; its error rates are declared (a correct lab fails (a) ~4.6% and (b) ~2.3% of evaluations; resolution ~±5% pooled Sharpe), and it reports the implied-Sharpe gap. The closed form itself stays approximate (√(2/π) under non-Gaussian y, the arcsine law, t's dispersion), so at other knobs or larger n a correct lab can still be refused. ~~**OWNER DECISION D-R11-1:** amend C-BT-11 v2 step 0's "within 2 se of its theory power".~~ **RATIFIED 2026-09-29 and APPLIED (ACIS-09 v1.9):** the owner chose "Equivalence in Sharpe (Recommended)". B0 (a) is now |implied Sharpe / theory − 1| ≤ 10% on n ≥ 2,000 (`B0_DELTA`, `B0_N`); on the lab it holds, a bridged lab reads ~50% low and is refused. Proof: `test_the_verdict_is_an_equivalence_band_at_the_declared_n`, `test_b0_fails_on_a_bridged_lab_its_power_is_lost`. The lead recommends an equivalence test in Sharpe units (|implied − theory| ≤ δ = 10%, at a declared n and configuration), with the closed form kept as a sanity bound; the alternative is a Monte-Carlo-calibrated reference power on an independent seed block, which is R1-C §4's own advice. Proof so far: `test_b0_holds_on_the_lab_detection_at_theory_power_and_the_null_at_alpha`.
  - **R11I-04 (medium-low) — the pooled theory used the wrong covariance.** FIXED: `LabSpec.common` = diff_scale²·ρ; theory 0.8555 against a Monte Carlo 0.8569. Proof: the B0 test pins 0.8555.
  - **R11I-05 (medium-low) — the lab's truth broke the oracle contract.** FIXED: `truth` is planted's drift on every kind (`generate(..., null=True)`; `permuted` keeps it in calendar order). Proof: `test_truth_is_the_drift_in_force_now_never_the_next_days_and_the_same_on_every_kind`.
  - **R11I-06 / E-R11-07 (low-medium / low) — the variance match is exact at one horizon only.** DECLARED and pinned (planted and permuted ratios, 20 s to 1 day). ~~**OWNER DECISION D-R11-2:**~~ **RATIFIED 2026-09-29 ("Reword (Recommended)") and APPLIED as ACIS-09 v1.9:** C-BT-12 now reads "each graded rule's decision (holding) horizon, matched exactly; planted and null differ at longer horizons by construction" (the lead's recommendation; the lab and the tick generator already comply under that reading). Owned with GAP-P12D-117.
  - **R11I-07 / E-R11-04 (low-medium / medium-low) — financing spread across the day leaked a carry edge.** FIXED: the previous night's accrual is a price step on the next day's open tick, at the instant the harness books it. Proof: `test_a_hold_straddling_the_rollover_earns_nothing_on_a_financed_null` (straddle and same-day holds both null; without the step the leak is detected).
  - **R11I-08 / E-R11-05 (low-medium) — units.** DECLARED: power statements are in volatility units (a fixed-notional grade earns ~10% less); a lab "year" is 252 weekdays while the calendar has ~261. Stated in `LabSpec`, `lab_source` and the tick generator's `drift_sigma`.
  - **R11I-09 (low) — the oracle-Sharpe test was a tautology.** FIXED: a realised-Sharpe test at 20 s and 300 s; planted specs whose shortest episode is under 10 ticks are refused. Proof: `test_the_no_foresight_oracle_realises_its_stated_sharpe_and_short_episodes_are_refused`.
  - **R11I-10 (low) — the positive control's PSR passed only on its seed.** FIXED: 12 folds on the pair (PSR 1.000 on seeds 7–11), a binding Sharpe bar of 4.0. Proof: `test_the_harness_recognises_the_oracle_on_planted_and_refuses_it_on_null`.
  - **R11I-11 (low) — the refusal checked r, not spot.** FIXED: spot and the intraday level are checked, and the knobs are bounded. Proof: `test_a_spot_return_at_or_below_minus_one_is_refused_daily_and_intraday`.
  - **R11I-12 (low) — the hygiene law was a denylist.** FIXED: an allowlist of modules and named imports, no dynamic imports, friction constants refused in any binding. Proof: `test_the_checker_catches_the_four_bypasses_the_audit_built`.
  - **R11I-13 (low) — the tape's last quote could miss the daily close.** FIXED: the last grid tick always publishes. Proof: `test_ticks_are_quotes_on_the_fx_clock_and_close_on_the_daily_bar`.
  - **R11I-14 (low) — read-path and ledger hygiene.** FIXED: stale Justification lines, superseded claims struck in CLAUDE.md / STATUS.md / TODO.md, and the red gate owned by [[GAP-C10-01]].
  - **E-R11-03 (medium-low) — the lab's true tail index was ~7.8.** FIXED: ν = 4.5, and the gate checks the model's index (min(ν, the GARCH Kesten root)), which refuses the old knobs. Proof: `test_2_tails_are_heavy_with_a_tail_index_between_two_and_five`.
  - **E-R11-06 (low-medium) — the lab's swap convention was not structural.** FIXED: `lab_config` forces `tom_next`. Proof: `test_the_lab_config_copies_a_declared_cost_block_and_touches_no_real_file`.
  - **E-R11-08 (low) — the DST claim was false.** FIXED: every FX day is 24 h, asserted on all 400 test days. Proof: `test_the_day_runs_rollover_to_rollover_and_never_touches_the_weekend`.
  - **E-R11-09 (low) — the harness's rollover calendar contradicts venues on gold, USD/CAD and holidays.** OWNED by [[GAP-P12D-93]] (the swap table and calendar in C8, R1.3); both instruments are disabled today.
- Justification: fixed 2026-09-29. The exact friction split was re-measured on the Google server (GAP-P12D-49); D-R11-1 and D-R11-2 were ratified and applied (ACIS-09 v1.9). Every other finding is fixed with a regression test, declared, or owned elsewhere.
- Proof: the tests named per finding above; `data/validation/cost_stress/20260929T163844Z-gce/ladder.json` for R11I-01.
- Residual: GAP-P12D-93 — E-R11-09, the harness's rollover calendar against venues on gold, USD/CAD and holidays.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-49]], [[GAP-P12D-105]], [[GAP-P12D-117]], [[GAP-P12D-124]], [[GAP-P12D-93]], [[GAP-C10-01]]

## GAP-P12D-118: the no-trade buffer is defined two incompatible ways, and needs portfolio state AGON lacks
- Severity: medium · Likelihood: high
- Status: open [impl — spec corrected v2.22; build R1.6 (the Governor's band)] (2026-09-25 — from the dual audit of master v2.21; triage in R1_AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-09-25.md)
- Area: master §2 row, §5.5, CLAUDE.md rule 5, ACIS-04 C-ALP-4
- Description: a dead zone around a zero forecast (§2, rule 5) against a band around the current position (§5.5, Carver). Under the first, an open position is KEPT when forecasts cancel (archive/AUDIT_R1_AMENDMENTS_INTERNAL_2026-09-25.md I10, archive/AUDIT_R1_AMENDMENTS_EXTERNAL_2026-09-25.md E8).
- **2026-10-02: the R1.6 interface RATIFIED and applied (master v2.27; ACIS-04 §4b, ACIS-05 §6.4: the Governor's band around the target); the build is next.**
- **R1.6 build progress 2026-10-02:** the band, the one lot rule and the practice cap are ONE function for RSIM and the Governor (`core/governor/target.py` `banded_target`; `test_rsim_trades_through_the_governors_function_and_its_practice_book_never_exceeds_one_lot`); the runtime wiring is next (TODO START HERE item 2).
- **R1.6 build 2026-10-02 (steps 3–4):** the runtime trades the band: the target path's 17:00 decision calls the same `banded_target` RSIM grades, and B0(d) parity holds decision by decision on a lab tape (`test_b0d_the_engines_decisions_equal_rsims_practice_book_on_a_lab_tape`, 360 decisions). The legacy policy's three as-built departures no longer apply to the target universe: its legacy signals are off there (R6-3, `test_a_runtime_from_the_shipped_config_wires_the_target_loop_and_silences_legacy`) and so is its max-hold exit (`test_the_legacy_max_hold_never_closes_a_target_universe_position`, R16I-09). Remaining departures: [[GAP-P12D-141]].
- Justification: open. Disposition: C11: Carver's band around the current position, owned by the Governor. APPLIED to the spec: the owner ratified the triage 2026-09-25 ("Ratify all"); the spec is corrected in master v2.22 and the deep specs. The build is owed; the as-built v2.20 behaviour stands until it lands.
- As-built parity (2026-09-30, [[GAP-P12D-132]] TAI-18): the runtime's position policy (`core/runtime/policy.py`, GAP-0075) departs from the harness book three ways, declared in its docstring: runtime-only pair-group exits, a target closed at the mark (the book fills at the target), and the max hold timed from the fill (the book's from `sized_at`). The band build aligns the book and the runtime; until then these three are this entry's.
- **Wording to ratify (found building RSIM, 2026-10-01):** master v2.22 §5.5 says the band is "±10% of the instrument's average position around the CURRENT position … a target outside it trades to the band's nearest edge", and calls it Carver's. Carver's code (pysystemtrade `buffer_method: forecast`, `buffer_trade_to_edge: True`, at `a62269d2`) puts the band around the TARGET (optimum ± 0.1·A) and trades the CURRENT position to that band's nearest edge. Both agree on WHEN no order is placed (|target − current| ≤ 0.1·A) but not on WHERE a trade ends: the literal reading moves the position only 0.1·A toward the target per day. RSIM builds Carver's (the spec names Carver as the authority; `test_the_buffer_keeps_inside_the_band_and_trades_to_its_nearest_edge`). OWNER: ratify the wording fix "a band of ±10% of the average position around the TARGET; a current position outside it trades to the band's nearest edge". **RATIFIED 2026-10-01 ("Ratify Carver's (Recommended)") and APPLIED as master v2.26 §5.5 and ACIS-04 v1.5.**
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-103]], [[GAP-P12D-132]]

## GAP-P12D-117: the synthetic-lab law has two holes — the variance match names no horizon, and the ±1-sign null has zero edge only in log units
- Severity: medium-high · Likelihood: certain
- Status: fixed (2026-09-29, after D-R11-2 was ratified and applied as ACIS-09 v1.9. Earlier the same day it was reopened by the R1.1 audit, R11I-06 / E-R11-07: the price-unit hole is closed in both generators, but the horizon hole is only DECLARED. The spec's "every horizon the lab's rules use" cannot be met by one scale for any persistent effect, and the tick generator's engine modules decide on 60 s bars where planted and permuted sit ~4% under the null. Owner decision D-R11-2 in GAP-P12D-125. Earlier the same day: both holes closed in the tick generator AND in the daily-first lab, R1.1 C-3. First raised 2026-09-25 by the dual audit of master v2.21; triage in R1_AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-09-25.md)
- Area: ACIS-09 v1.7 C-BT-12, tests/backtest/synthetic_market.py
- Description: the confounded generator passes "1 ± 0.05" at its 20 s step (1.023) while its daily ratio is 14.7; and exp-built prices give a long-biased rule σ²/2 a day (SR +0.31 at NATGAS-like volatility) (archive/AUDIT_R1_AMENDMENTS_EXTERNAL_2026-09-25.md E3, E7, seeded).
- Justification: fixed 2026-09-29 (D-R11-2 applied; the old engine's composite horizon is a declared deviation, retired with the engine at R1.7). Disposition: C10: match at the decision horizon by construction; flip arithmetic increments, or add antithetic paths; add a price-unit null test. APPLIED to the spec: the owner ratified the triage 2026-09-25 ("Ratify all"); the spec is corrected in master v2.22 and the deep specs.
  - **Built 2026-09-29 in the tick generator:**
    - the match is at `match_seconds`, the graded rule's decision horizon, by construction (see [[GAP-P12D-90]]);
    - every kind's prices COMPOUND SIMPLE RETURNS: the zero-mean draw is the simple return r, and mid_k = mid_(k-1)·(1 + r_k). So E[Δmid | the past] = 0, the permuted null's sign flips act on arithmetic increments, and neither null pays a long bias in price units, the units the harness books. (The first cut, the same day, was additive: first · (1 + running sum). It could cross zero at high volatility, so it was replaced before any daily lab was built on it; one law now serves both generators);
    - the log-unit drift is then −σ²/2 a unit time: a declared deviation, ~1e-5 a day at FX volatility, in units nothing grades;
    - a simple return ≤ −1 is refused, never floored.
  - **For the daily lab (C-3):** R1-C's prototype builds the mid as the exponential of the cumulative return, which is exactly this hole. It takes the same compounded-simple-return law, which stays positive at any volatility the lab uses.
- Proof: `test_prices_compound_simple_returns_so_a_long_bias_earns_nothing_in_price_units` (the identity mid_k = mid_(k-1)·(1 + r_k); a buy-and-hold's price P&L centres on 0 over 2,000 paths at 2% daily vol; the same draws built as exp(Σ) earn σ²T/2 more, at t > 10), `test_a_simple_return_at_or_below_minus_one_is_refused`, `test_planted_matches_the_null_variance_exactly_at_the_decision_horizon`. The first cut's tests are former `test_prices_are_arithmetic_so_a_long_bias_earns_nothing_in_price_units` and former `test_a_path_that_would_cross_zero_is_refused`.
- Residual: none for the lab — D-R11-2 was ratified and applied (ACIS-09 v1.9: exact at each graded rule's decision horizon; longer horizons differ by construction). The ONE declared deviation left is the tick generator grading the old ENGINE, a composite of 60 s modules and daily TSMOM that no single scale can match at both; it is matched at one weekday, and its 60 s modules see planted and permuted ~4% quieter than null. That engine is retired by R1.7. The measured ratios are pinned by `test_at_the_default_strength_no_horizon_tells_planted_from_null_by_volatility` (planted AND permuted). Price-unit half: the daily lab (`tests/backtest/lab_market.py`) takes the same law: prices compound the zero-mean simple return; the planted market's MODEL variance of y over the decision horizon (one weekday) equals the null's by construction (scale² · (Var m + other) = other, common random numbers); the permuted control flips whole days' arithmetic increments. Proof there: `test_planted_is_the_nulls_noise_plus_the_drift_times_one_exact_scale`, `test_a_long_bias_earns_nothing_in_price_units_on_either_null` (the same draws built as exp(sum r), R1-C's prototype law, earn the convexity at t > 10), `test_permuting_keeps_every_day_and_its_cross_section_and_is_a_martingale`.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-90]], [[GAP-P12D-91]], [[GAP-P12D-103]], [[GAP-P12D-124]]

## GAP-P12D-116: the TREND family's "MOP-2012 12-month sign" baseline points to a 3-horizon SPOT vote, not MOP's 12-month EXCESS-return sign
- Severity: medium · Likelihood: certain
- Status: fixed (2026-10-01, R1.3; spec corrected v2.22) (2026-09-25 — from the dual audit of master v2.21; triage in R1_AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-09-25.md)
- Area: master v2.21 §5.6, ACIS-04 v1.3 §6.6, core/signals/canonical.py, GAP-0073
- Description: MOP 2012 signs the 12-month excess return (eq. 5; App. A.3). On the repo's rates, spot and excess 12-month signs disagree on 3.0–12.1% of days across FX pairs, 2013–2025. So GAP-0073's "immaterial" is false (archive/AUDIT_R1_AMENDMENTS_EXTERNAL_2026-09-25.md E6).
- Justification: fixed. `rules.mop_12m` signs the 12-month EXCESS return at each month's last weekday (a calendar fact) and holds it a month; `rules.mop_vol` is MOP's ex-ante estimate (centre of mass 60 days); the record `trend_mop12_v1` declares its four departures. GAP-0073's "immaterial" note is withdrawn in TSMOM's declared deviations (`core/signals/canonical.py`); the 3-horizon vote stays a separate member.
- Proof: `test_mop_signs_the_twelve_month_excess_return_at_each_month_end_and_holds_it`, `test_month_end_is_a_calendar_fact`, `test_every_departure_the_code_makes_is_declared`, `test_a_forecast_never_reads_past_its_decision_day`.
- Residual: none — both parts are done: the excess-return price for real instruments is RSIM's real-data source (GAP-P12D-136 item 1), and the corrected TSMOM declaration is the lineage successor `tsmom_slow_v2` (GAP-P12D-136 item 5, `test_a_corrected_record_is_a_lineage_successor_and_the_parent_stays_as_written`); a store holding v1 keeps it as written, by ACIS-11 §6.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-71]], [[GAP-P12D-103]], [[GAP-P12D-136]]

## GAP-P12D-115: the "cost ≤ ⅓ of pre-cost Sharpe" filter is attributed to Carver but departs from his speed limit twice
- Severity: medium · Likelihood: high
- Status: fixed (2026-10-01, R1.3; spec corrected v2.22) (2026-09-25 — from the dual audit of master v2.21; triage in R1_AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-09-25.md)
- Area: master v2.21 §5.6, ACIS-09 v1.7 stage A
- Description: Carver caps TURNOVER cost at ~0.13 SR units against a fixed expected Sharpe. The spec adds swap, which is mostly carry return for FX, and stage A uses a REALISED per-instrument Sharpe: an unregistered look (archive/AUDIT_R1_AMENDMENTS_EXTERNAL_2026-09-25.md E5).
- Justification: fixed. `tests/backtest/cost_filters.py`: Carver's speed limit, (forecast turnover + rolls) × the cost per trade in SR units <= 0.10 (pysystemtrade `a62269d2` definitions: `turnover(forecast, 10)` and `calculate_sr_cost`; his blog's two figures 0.13/0.10, the stricter taken), and swap viability, markup × time in market / annual vol <= 0.10 (1/3 of a declared 0.30). Each member's turnover and time in market are FIXED inputs measured on the synthetic null (P&L-blind, no real look; `scripts/r1_lab_turnover.py`), beside Carver's published S&P 500 turnovers; the filter takes the larger.
- Proof: `test_turnover_is_carvers_mean_absolute_change_of_f_over_10_annualised`, `test_the_speed_limit_admits_at_or_below_point_ten`, `test_swap_viability_divides_the_markup_by_vol_at_the_time_in_market`, `test_the_filter_turnover_is_the_larger_of_the_lab_and_carvers_figure`.
- Residual: GAP-P12D-136 — the per-instrument admission run on the real candles (spreads at the execution slot and vol, P&L-blind) at the first look's registration.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-93]], [[GAP-P12D-103]], [[GAP-P12D-136]]

## GAP-P12D-111: stage A controls family-wise error at ~2α, not α; its looks go unadjusted; and α restarts every quarter
- Severity: high · Likelihood: certain
- Status: fixed (2026-10-03, R1.4: serial gatekeeping, α/k per look and the spending schedule are built) (2026-09-25 — from the dual audit of master v2.21; triage in R1_AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-09-25.md)
- Area: ACIS-09 v1.7 C-BT-11 v2 stage A
- Description: a fixed-sequence chain plus a parallel Holm set, each at α, gives FWER 0.081–0.096 (seeded; ≈ 0.0975 for independent sets, lead arithmetic). "≤ 3 looks per family" has no correction. The quarterly stream has no lifetime budget (archive/AUDIT_R1_AMENDMENTS_INTERNAL_2026-09-25.md I13, archive/AUDIT_R1_AMENDMENTS_EXTERNAL_2026-09-25.md E2, FDA Multiple Endpoints guidance 2022 §§5, 7).
- **Fix 2026-10-03:** `tests/backtest/verdict.py` `cycle_verdict`: the chain's members are tested in the registered order at the cycle's α and testing stops at the first member that has not passed; a member's p is k × its best look's one-sided HAC p (each look at α/k, k the declared budget); the Holm set is tested only after the whole chain passed, Holm at the cycle's α; an explored member, a pending look or a broken register fails closed. The order-before-any-look rule and the spending schedule (0.03, then half the unspent remainder, one cycle a UTC quarter) were built at R1.2 (`look_claims.cycle_line`).
- Residual: GAP-P12D-127 — each stage-A look does not yet name its cycle and look index (R12I-04), so a variant listed in two cycles would count its looks in both; that lands before real-data looks open.
- Justification: fixed. Disposition C6 + D-d (owner-ratified 2026-09-25) built as specified; GAP-P12D-140 (1) may still re-rule cycle 1's allocation before its row is registered.
- Linked: test_the_chain_passes_in_order_and_stops_at_the_first_member_that_has_not_passed, test_each_look_is_tested_at_alpha_over_k, test_the_holm_set_is_tested_only_after_the_whole_chain_passed, test_holm_passes_the_strong_member_first_and_then_tests_the_next_at_the_full_alpha, test_an_explored_member_a_pending_look_and_a_broken_register_fail_closed, [[GAP-P12D-102]], [[GAP-P12D-121]], [[GAP-P12D-103]]

## GAP-P12D-105: the applied claim ladder dropped B0, "machinery proven first", which the ratified A6 draft required
- Severity: high · Likelihood: medium
- Status: mitigated (2026-09-29 — the B0 MACHINERY is built, R1.1: `tests/backtest/lab_calibration.py` measures a detector's null false-detect rate and planted power against theory on the daily lab; not yet wired into the verdict, R1.4. Raised 2026-09-25 by the dual audit of master v2.21; triage in R1_AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-09-25.md)
- Area: master v2.21 §14 rung 1, ACIS-09 C-BT-11 v2
- Description: R1_LEAD_REVIEW's ratified A6 made rung 1 = B0–B5. The applied text has no lab-calibration precondition, which is the GAP-P12D-37 class: a grader that is itself wrong (archive/AUDIT_R1_AMENDMENTS_INTERNAL_2026-09-25.md I2).
- Justification: mitigated. Disposition: C2: restore B0 as a precondition of any real-data verdict. APPLIED to the spec: the owner ratified the triage 2026-09-25 ("Ratify all"); the spec is corrected in master v2.22 and the deep specs. The build is owed; the as-built v2.20 behaviour stands until it lands.
- Built 2026-09-29 (the Google compute server; 400 paths of 2.8 graded years, EWMAC 16/64 pooled over 20 instruments):
  - **B0 (a) and (b) HOLD on the lab:** planted detection **37.8%** against a theory power of 39.8% (pooled theory Sharpe 0.828, within 2 se); null false-detect **5.5%** (limit alpha + 2 se = 7.2%).
  - **The check has power:** the same paths demeaned per instrument (GAP-P12D-91's defect) detect the planted trend at **18.2%** and B0 refuses them; a detector that always finds an edge is refused on its false-detect rate.
  - **Where the rest of B0 lives:** (c) a leaky oracle refused by structure is the leakage suite ([[GAP-P12D-65]]); (e) planted variance-matched to its null is pinned in both generators ([[GAP-P12D-90]], [[GAP-P12D-117]]); (d) the research simulator equal to the engine on lab tapes needs RSIM (R1.3) and the engine reconciliation (R1.6).
  - **The detector is a placeholder:** the plain one-sided t-test. R1.4's stage-A HAC bound ([[GAP-P12D-121]]) must pass through `calibrate` before it binds.
- Proof: `test_b0_holds_on_the_lab_detection_at_theory_power_and_the_null_at_alpha`, `test_b0_fails_on_a_bridged_lab_its_power_is_lost`, `test_b0_fails_a_detector_that_always_finds_an_edge`, former `test_the_verdict_arithmetic_is_binomial` (replaced by `test_the_verdict_is_an_equivalence_band_at_the_declared_n`, ACIS-09 v1.9).
- Audit 2026-09-29 (GAP-P12D-125, R11I-03/04, E-R11-01): the rule now matches its closed form (sign-EWMAC on y), the pooled theory uses the lab's true covariance (0.8555), and the block is the declared B0_N = 2,000 seeds; error rates are declared and the implied-Sharpe gap reported. The criterion became an equivalence test in Sharpe units (±10% at n ≥ 2,000), owner-ratified D-R11-1 and applied as ACIS-09 v1.9 the same day.
- Residual: GAP-P12D-105 — still owns wiring B0 into `passed` (R1.4) with all five conditions at the same `recon_commit`, including (d), which waits on R1.3/R1.6.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-37]], [[GAP-P12D-103]]

## GAP-P12D-104: C-BT-11 v2 redefines `passed`, the field the live gate reads, without the §14 Max DD/SPX gates or C-BT-2/4/7
- Severity: high · Likelihood: medium
- Status: fixed (2026-10-03, R1.4: `report.passed` is C-BT-11 v2's; built to the spec as corrected in v2.22) (2026-09-25 — from the dual audit of master v2.21; triage in R1_AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-09-25.md)
- Area: ACIS-09 v1.7 C-BT-11 v2 and its live-gate paragraph; ACIS-08; monitoring/mode.py
- Description: a built verdict would pass a 30% drawdown or a guardrail-hash mismatch to the human token (the live gate reads `passed`: ACIS-09's live-gate paragraph, `mode.py`, verified by the lead). A2 ratified replacing only the DSR clause (archive/AUDIT_R1_AMENDMENTS_INTERNAL_2026-09-25.md I1).
- **Fix 2026-10-03:** `tests/backtest/report.py`: `passed` = a `StageRecord` that holds (B0, stages A and B passed, stage C did not kill) AND the two §14 gates (`GATES`: Max DD < 15%, SPX correlation < 0.25) AND C-BT-2 (≥ 2 years, > 1 fold, ≥ 30 trades), C-BT-4 and C-BT-7. A report without a stage record never passes. The five retired §14 rows stay in `targets_met`, reported and never gated; the Deflated Sharpe and trial count are informational. The legacy oracle's `non_dsr_failures` stays the v2.20 lab criterion its controls were calibrated on (GAP-P12D-31/37), and a test pins it as never looser than `passed`. The live gate is unchanged: production wires `validation_record_ok=lambda: False` (`core/runtime/wiring.py`), and the rung-2 record does not exist yet.
- Residual: GAP-P12D-149 — the stage record's producers (B0's record, stage B, stage C, the per-account re-grade); until they exist every report's `passed` is False.
- Justification: fixed. Disposition C1 (owner-ratified 2026-09-25, "Ratify all") is built as specified.

## GAP-P12D-103: the dual audit of the applied R1 amendments (master v2.21, commit 271d8e0)
- Severity: high · Likelihood: high
- Status: fixed (2026-09-25 — both lenses' 37 findings TRIAGED in R1_AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-09-25.md and RATIFIED by the owner ("Ratify all"); corrections C1–C15 and decisions D-a..D-g APPLIED as master v2.22, ACIS-02 v1.4, ACIS-03 v2.1, ACIS-04 v1.4, ACIS-08 v1.3, ACIS-09 v1.8, ACIS-11 v1.2, OOS_VALIDATION.md and CLAUDE.md rule 5)
- Area: ACIS_SPEC.md v2.21 (§2, §4, §5, §5.5, §14), specs/ACIS-03 v2.0, ACIS-04 v1.3, ACIS-09 v1.7 (C-BT-11 v2, C-BT-12), OOS_VALIDATION.md, CLAUDE.md rule 5
- Description: the guard VALUES are untouched (no `config/` change; master §6–§9.5 byte-identical; the confidence veto kept). But the applied text fails three of its own claims. The findings, verified by the lead where marked:
  - **I1 (high, verified):** C-BT-11 v2 defines `passed` as stages A–C only, while ACIS-09's live gate and `monitoring/mode.py` read `passed`. So a built verdict would pass a 30% drawdown, or a guardrail-hash mismatch, to the human token. A2 ratified replacing only the DSR clause.
  - **I2 (high):** the ratified A6 made rung 1 = B0–B5, but the applied text dropped B0 ("machinery proven first": the lab calibration).
  - **I3 (high):** "validated", the new `tradeable=False` trigger's predicate, is defined nowhere. Read as rung 1, an LLM-originated family can never trade, so it can never earn the forward record stage D needs; and the as-built demo trades unvalidated sets.
  - **I4 (high, verified):** the staging rule is "unchanged", but its only implementation (`param_library.py`: `PromotionForbiddenError`, `PositionGate`, stage/promote/on_boundary) is on the retirement list. `core/signals/provenance.py:22` imports it.
  - **I5 (high, verified):** §4's "NVMA's one effect on trading is the veto" contradicts the unamended §6 regime sizing factor (`capital_governor.py:292-303`), C4, §3.6 and ACIS-02. Built literally, it is an unannounced size-UP (1/regime_f). One trigger (no params for the regime) was REPLACED, not kept.
  - **I6 (medium-high, verified):** the stage-D bettors are mis-specified. E2 subtracts a Sharpe ratio from a return; σ* has no units; the clip contradicts "never clipped"; and "E2 silent ≥ 3 months" is met by 99.8% of dead strategies. PLAN_R1's "25% killed by 1 year" is the FREEZE figure.
  - **I7 (medium-high):** the family verdict on the research universe is not the per-account grade §14/C-BT-7 still require.
  - **I8 (medium-high):** "R1.7" is defined only in PLAN_R1, whose list also retires marginal-alpha, the fragmenter and C4, which v2.21 never amended.
  - **I9–I14 (medium):**
    - I9: rung 2 needs the practice mode ([[GAP-P12D-69]]);
    - I10: two incompatible no-trade buffers, and the buffer needs portfolio state AGON lacks;
    - I11: netting plus the cap leaves a pair leg alone;
    - I12: staging plus continuous positions means promotion never happens;
    - I13: stage A's α split, its look correction and the registration of the order are unstated (union FWER ≈ 0.098);
    - I14: "stricter meanwhile" is false as a universal claim.
  - **I15–I19 (low to low-medium):**
    - I15: rounding positions vs rounding orders;
    - I16: the embargo and config do not fit a fixed family;
    - I17: C9 per member and the fixed-family plateau check are uncomputable as written;
    - F18 (verified): the held-out draw reproduces only with slash spellings ("XCU/USD");
    - I19: read-path residue.
- Justification: open until the triage is applied. Triage (R1_AUDIT_TRIAGE_2026-09-25.md) is written; originally: one consolidated correction pass restoring the ratified intent (tightening), plus owner decisions for I3 (what "validated" means per mode), I5 (whether regime_f leaves sizing, a size-up) and I12 (promotion under netting; the staging rule is law). Until then, no R1.x build starts, and the as-built v2.20 behaviour stands.
- Proof: master v2.22 changelog (ACIS_SPEC.md), ACIS-09 v1.8 C-BT-11 v2 and C-BT-12, `test_spec_constants` still green.
- Residual: GAP-P12D-104..123 — each owns its build (open [impl]); GAP-P12D-109 and GAP-P12D-113 are fixed in the spec.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-89]], [[GAP-P12D-96]], [[GAP-P12D-101]], [[GAP-P12D-102]], [[GAP-P12D-69]]

## GAP-P12D-102: even swap-free, the R1 flagship family (pooled trend, "D1") most likely fails the plan's own development bar
- Severity: high · Likelihood: high
- Status: open [impl — RATIFIED 2026-09-25 (owner: "i ratify all your recommendations for R1-D1 to D14") and the spec APPLIED: ACIS-09 v1.7 C-BT-11 v2 stage A is fixed-sequence; built with the verdict module, R1.4] (2026-09-25 — found by the lead reviewing PLAN_R1; `archive/r1_lead_probes/d1_power.py` → `.out`)
- Area: PLAN_R1 §6 B2 and its A2 (C-BT-11 v2); the testing procedure of stage A
- Description: PLAN_R1 estimates D1's net portfolio Sharpe at +0.32..+0.79 swap-free (−0.15..+0.32 at FTMO's swaps). Its own stage-A bar is SR̂ > 0.81 (Holm, K ≤ 10, Y = 10), or 0.74 at K = 5. With the plan's own se = 1/√Y, D1 passes 6% / 21% / 47% of the time at the low / mid / high swap-free estimate, and 1% at FTMO swaps. A pre-registered fixed-sequence order (D1 first, at the full α; the next family only if it passes) controls family-wise error exactly as Holm does and lowers D1's bar to 0.50 at Y = 11 (fixed families need no 2-year burn-in): 28% / 58% / 84%.
- Justification: open. Recommendation in R1_LEAD_REVIEW_2026-09-25.md §2 X2 and A2: fixed-sequence for the first cycle; Holm only among families outside the order. The owner rules with A2.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-89]], [[GAP-P12D-94]], [[GAP-P12D-67]]

## GAP-P12D-101: the R1 plan never models the venue's minimum lot — at the owner's account size most of its breadth cannot be held
- Severity: high · Likelihood: certain
- Status: open [impl — R1-D14 RATIFIED 2026-09-25 (owner: "i ratify all your recommendations for R1-D1 to D14") and the spec APPLIED: lot rounding in master v2.21 §5 C8 and the live-eligibility rule in OOS_VALIDATION.md; the account size / vol target is decided WITH R1-D9 at the first real-data look] (2026-09-25 — found by the lead; `archive/r1_lead_probes/lot_granularity.py` → `.out`, FTMO contract sizes from `archive/r1_lead_probes/ftmo_symbols_2026-09-25.json`; the 0.01-lot minimum is an ASSUMPTION, since the JSON carries no min-volume field)
- Area: PLAN_R1 A3/A5 (breadth, IDM ≈ 2.4–2.5, N_eff ≈ 6), the research simulator (RSIM), ACIS-06 C-EXE-6 lot rounding, [[GAP-P12D-61]]
- Description: Carver sizing (capital × vol target × weight × IDM) with ACIS's round-down to lot steps, class-equal weights over the 23 explored instruments:
  - at $6,000 and a 10% vol target, 15 of 23 can NEVER hold one minimum lot (all 13 FX pairs, gold, silver);
  - at $10,000 and 10%, 6 FX pairs never can, and the rest only at |forecast| ≥ 13–20, i.e. mostly flat;
  - at $10,000 and 20%, or $25,000 and 10%, all can.

  The instruments fine-grained enough to hold are the index CFDs, which carry FTMO's HIGHEST swap markups (3.6–5%/yr). So the swap regime and the account size are one decision. And unless RSIM rounds to lot steps exactly as execution does, the graded book is not the tradeable book (the GAP-P12D-36 class), and B4 fails by construction.
- **J1 (2026-10-02, COMPLETE; `gs://acis-compute-0929-data/runs/2026-10-02-j1/`, 1,000 lab seeds a cell, swap-free, 1,000-unit lots):** stage A's false-pass rate on the null stays at or below α 0.03 in every cell (1.7–2.3%). Days D1 holds a position, and its pass rate on planted trends (Sharpe 0.5 half-life 40 / Sharpe 1.0 half-life 40 / Sharpe 0.5 half-life 160): reference with no lot rule 100%, 19.2 / 97.9 / 64.1%; **$10k at 10% (the demo's R6-5) 20–25%, 10.9 / 77.8 / 37.3%**; $10k at 20% 45–51%, 16.2 / 95.4 / 55.5%; $25k at 10% 53–59%, 17.1 / 94.8 / 56.8%; $100k at 10% 86–88%, 19.5 / 98.0 / 64.4%; $100k at 20% 93–94%, 19.7 / 98.1 / 64.5%. So the minimum lot costs about 40% of stage A's power at $10k / 10%; doubling the vol target or a $25k account recovers most of it, and $100k matches the reference. This is the R1-D14 evidence; the demo's own vol target is raised with [[GAP-P12D-142]].
- **Owner ruling 2026-10-02 (from J1):** the demo trades at a 20% vol target on its $10k capital_ref ("20% (Recommended)"; ACIS-05 v1.8). R1-D14 for a funded account stays open: J1's table is its evidence.
- Justification: open. (1) [impl] RSIM must round exactly as C-EXE-6 does. (2) [spec, A5] an instrument is live-eligible on an account only if one minimum lot is ≤ its risk budget at |forecast| = 10 there. (3) [owner, R1-D14] account size and vol target are decided with R1-D9, at the first real-data look.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-94]], [[GAP-P12D-57]], [[GAP-P12D-61]], [[GAP-P12D-102]]

## GAP-P12D-100: a pooled rule must be graded on its portfolio daily series — the stacked instrument × day panel passes a zero-edge rule 9.5–32% of the time
- Severity: high · Likelihood: medium
- Status: fixed (2026-10-03, R1.4: the verdict reads only the stored portfolio vector) (2026-09-25 — from PLAN_R1 §9 #12, R1-A simulation; not re-run by the local lead)
- Area: the verdict module (PLAN_R1 R1.4), C-BT-11 v2 (A2)
- Description: pooling instruments into one panel and testing the stacked instrument-days treats correlated observations as independent. R1-A measured 9.5–32% false passes on a zero-edge rule, against 3.8–6.0% for the portfolio daily series.
- **Fix 2026-10-03:** `tests/backtest/verdict.py` grades a member only through `stage_a.from_register`, which reads the look's stored daily vector; `evaluate()` stores RSIM's book-level `daily` (one net return per day), and `store_vector` refuses a repeated date, so a stacked instrument × day panel cannot be stored, let alone graded.
- Residual: none — the graded unit is structural: one vector per look, keyed by strictly increasing dates.
- Justification: fixed; the 9.5–32% figure is PLAN_R1's and was not re-run (it no longer matters: the panel form cannot reach the grader).
- Linked: test_a_stacked_instrument_day_panel_cannot_be_stored_as_the_graded_vector, [[GAP-P12D-89]], [[GAP-P12D-98]]

## GAP-P12D-99: `core/signals/indicators.py` imports its indicators from `core/optimizer/fitness.py` — they must move before QIEA can be retired
- Severity: low · Likelihood: certain
- Status: open [impl] (2026-09-25 — from PLAN_R1 §9 #11; verified by the lead at `core/signals/indicators.py:16`)
- Area: core/signals/indicators.py, core/optimizer/fitness.py (`atr_series`, `ema_series`, `rsi_series`)
- Description: the signal layer re-exports three indicator functions defined in the optimizer's fitness module. Retiring `fitness.py` (PLAN_R1 A4 / R1.7) would break the signal layer unless they move first.
- Justification: open; a pure move, behaviour-identical, proven by the existing indicator tests. It needs no ratification.
- **Sequencing (2026-10-01, R1.3):** the move lands IN R1.7's retirement commit, not before. The import law lets `signals` import `optimizer` but not the reverse (`tests/unit/test_import_contract.py` ALLOWED), so moving the three functions into `core/signals/indicators.py` while `fitness.py` still imports them would break the law or need a duplicate. Nothing in R1.3 depends on them: the TREND rules use Carver's adjusted EWMA (`core/signals/ewm.py`), which `fitness.ema_series` does not implement.

## GAP-P12D-98: the look register stores per-trade returns, not the daily net-return vector a portfolio-level verdict needs
- Severity: high · Likelihood: certain
- Status: mitigated (2026-09-29, R1.2 built in-session: register v2. `tests/backtest/look_claims.py` + `look_register.py`. The single `evaluate()` entry point waits on RSIM, R1.3. First raised 2026-09-25 from PLAN_R1 §9 #10: after merge 3 each grade recorded per-trade returns only)
- Area: tests/backtest/look_register.py (`record_outcome`), the verdict module
- Description: search-aware tests over the register (Holm or fixed-sequence on family portfolio series, SPA/StepM, CPCV/PBO for explored families) need each registered run's DAILY portfolio net-return vector, content-addressed. The register now keeps pass fields and per-trade returns only. It also lacks the declared-search-space hash, origin + model cutoff, cost-map hash and universe id that PLAN_R1 R1.2 lists.
- Justification: mitigated; additive fields, tightening only, no ratification needed.
- Built 2026-09-29 (R1.2):
  - **The claim.** A real-data look REFUSES to register without its claim (`look_claims.Claim`). The claim holds: the declared search space (grid, formula, instrument list; canonical-JSON sha256), the variant and its `variant_hash` (rule, parameters, combination and universe, never the family's name), the `universe_id`, the origin (literature + dated reference / human / LLM + model + knowledge cutoff), the simulator, the `recon_commit` at which B0 held, and the cost-map hash (every graded instrument's C8 block, spread limit, swap convention, stress and floor).
  - **LLM repeats.** An LLM plan that repeats a registered `variant_hash` is refused (§5.6, R1-D13).
  - **Cycle rows.** A cycle's testing order is a `kind: cycle` line in the same hash chain: at most 5 distinct family-versions, alpha in (0, 0.05], a unique id, at most one cycle a calendar quarter. Counting skips cycle rows.
  - **The daily vector.** `record_outcome(..., daily=(dates, returns))` stores the portfolio's daily net-return vector once, content-addressed, under `vectors/`; the outcome carries its hash, and `load_vector` refuses an edited file.
  - **Housekeeping.** `begin_grade` moved to `tests/backtest/look_gate.py` (A12 budget: the register is 290 lines). `scripts/run_oos_validation.py` now takes `--origin` and `--recon-commit`.
- Proof: `test_a_real_look_must_carry_its_own_familys_claim_and_the_line_records_it`, `test_the_declared_space_is_hashed_and_any_change_to_it_changes_the_hash`, `test_an_origin_records_what_the_contamination_law_needs`, `test_an_llm_variant_is_refused_again_under_any_origin` (former `test_an_llm_plan_that_repeats_a_registered_variant_is_refused`), `test_the_cost_map_hash_separates_grades_priced_differently`, `test_a_cycle_orders_unlooked_variants_at_the_spending_alpha_one_a_utc_quarter` (former `test_a_cycle_row_is_a_chain_line_that_counting_skips_and_that_obeys_its_rules`), `test_the_daily_vector_is_stored_once_by_its_hash_and_an_edit_is_caught`, `test_the_real_data_script_writes_its_line_before_it_grades`.
- Residual: GAP-P12D-98 — still owns the single `evaluate(plan, tier, span)` entry point, the only producer of a graded number: it writes the claim, runs RSIM, stores the vector and feeds `ProvenanceStore.record_attempt`. It is built with RSIM, R1.3.
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-64]], [[GAP-P12D-89]], [[GAP-P12D-100]]

## GAP-P12D-97: the holdout kill at SR̂ < 0 wrongly kills 18–32% of real edges
- Severity: medium · Likelihood: high
- Status: open [impl — RATIFIED 2026-09-25 (owner: "i ratify all your recommendations for R1-D1 to D14") and the spec APPLIED: ACIS-09 v1.7 C-BT-11 v2 stage C kills at t < −0.5] (2026-09-25 — from PLAN_R1 §9 #9, R1-A simulation; not re-run by the local lead)
- Area: the kill rule for the held-out instruments and the 2024–25 holdout (VALIDATOR K4)
- Description: with two years of holdout, a true edge frequently shows a negative sample Sharpe. Killing at SR̂ < 0 throws away 18–32% of real edges; killing at t < −0.5 keeps the kill's power against false claims while sparing most real ones.
- Justification: open; decided with A2 (R1-D2).
- Linked: [[GAP-P12D-89]]

- Severity: high · Likelihood: certain
